```
CINEMATIC SHORT FILM, 46 sec, 16:9, photorealistic Hollywood sci-fi action, dystopian highway apocalypse. Cold steel-grey sky, burning orange explosions, volumetric god rays, anamorphic lens, film grain. HERO: muscular woman, curly dark hair, olive tank top, black jeans and boots, fierce expression. BIKE: matte-black futuristic armored motorcycle, twin fat front wheels, white glowing headlights, mounted cannon.

10 (22-23s): Frontal tracking, hero bursts out from under a dark overpass, smoke columns behind.
11 (24-26s): Driver POV down the highway, collapsed overpass and debris ahead, lone black car in the lane.
12 (27-28s): Wide side shot, bike launches off a broken concrete slab, front wheel high.
13 (29-33s): Slow-motion low-angle, hero and bike airborne against dramatic clouds and god rays, red taillight glowing.
14 (34-36s): Close-up, rear wheel hits a broken slab and explodes in a fireball, sparks and debris, slow motion.
15 (37-38s): Wide ground-level, debris-covered highway, overturned burning car, tiny hero falling through the sky.
16 (39-42s): Hero lands and skids into a low crouch, one knee and hand down, fire behind her.
17 (43-46s): Medium close-up, she rises, fierce eyes, wall of orange fire exploding behind her, freeze on hero pose.

MOTION: fast tracking, whip pans, slow-motion airborne beat, handheld shake on explosions, realistic physics, consistent character and bike design.

NEGATIVE PROMPT: cartoon, anime, CGI look, plastic skin, deformed face, bad hands, extra limbs, extra wheels, changing bike design, inconsistent character, blurry, low resolution, flicker, morphing, warped anatomy, text, subtitles, watermark, logo, oversaturated, duplicate characters, unnatural motion
```

### A girl's fireball leaves an Edo-village samurai in shock

> Bro wasn't ready for that fireball 😂 Seedance 2.5 on @renoiseai @renoiseaijp prompt Create an ultra-realistic, high-budget, 20-second cinematic Japanese fantas…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/4f00e1d42e40.jpg" width="600" alt="A girl's fireball leaves an Edo-village samurai in shock">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-bro-wasn-t-ready-for-that-fireball-78c69ad906e1?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Just_sharon7 | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Just_sharon7/status/2118644163008663764) | **公開日:** 2026-11-06 | **ヒート:** 51

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（168 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Bro wasn't ready for that fireball 😂

Seedance 2.5 on @renoiseai @renoiseaijp 

prompt

Create an ultra-realistic, high-budget, 20-second cinematic Japanese fantasy action sequence set in a traditional Edo-period Japanese village. The scene combines breathtaking martial arts choreography, explosive supernatural fire effects, intense facial expressions, dramatic slow-motion moments, and an unexpected comedic ending.

The video must look like a live-action Japanese fantasy blockbuster, with realistic human physics, natural skin textures, detailed costumes, authentic architecture, cinematic camera movements, and seamless visual effects.

MAIN CHARACTERS
Character 1 — Young Japanese Samurai
 @[Image 2](image_2)
A Japanese katana rests at his waist.
His personality is initially confident, serious, and intimidating, but transforms into complete shock and confusion after the supernatural attack.
Maintain identical facial features, hairstyle, costume, body proportions, and katana placement throughout the video.

Character 2 @[Image 1](image_1)
Her hair flows naturally during every movement, reacting realistically to sudden acceleration, wind, and explosions.

She possesses extraordinary agility, lightning-fast reflexes, and supernatural fire-manipulation abilities.
Her expression transitions from calm confidence to fierce determination, then to surprise at the unexpected result.

ENVIRONMENT
A historical Japanese village surrounded by distant misty mountains.
Traditional wooden buildings with dark tiled roofs line both sides of a wide dirt street.
Weathered wooden shopfronts, bamboo poles, market stalls, hanging fabric banners, and scattered wooden crates create an authentic atmosphere.
The ground is dusty and uneven, with small stones and patches of mud.

Lighting is natural late-afternoon daylight beneath a slightly overcast sky.
The atmosphere initially feels peaceful but becomes chaotic when supernatural flames erupt.
All environmental elements must remain spatially consistent throughout the sequence.

SHOT-BY-SHOT CINEMATIC SEQUENCE
SHOT 1 — THE UNEXPECTED ATTACK (0:00–0:03)
Medium-wide cinematic tracking shot.
The @[Image 1](image_1) woman walks past the Japanese samurai along the village street.
The samurai stands beside a traditional wooden building, watching her with a confident expression.
Without warning, he @[Image 2](image_2) launches a powerful sideways kick toward her upper body.
His kimono fabric whips through the air as his leg extends.
The woman reacts instantly.
She twists her shoulders, bends backward, and drops into a low evasive movement, narrowly avoiding the kick.
Her long hair whips dramatically across the frame.
Camera movement: Smooth lateral tracking transitioning into a rapid handheld push-in.
Sound: Sharp cloth movement, powerful kick whoosh, sudden impact-style bass accent.
SHOT 2 — ACROBATIC EVASION (0:03–0:06)
Low-angle dynamic action shot.
The woman continues her evasive movement, lowering her body close to the dusty ground.
Her blazer and pleated skirt respond naturally to the motion.
She rotates her torso, extends one arm, and begins a powerful counterattack.
Her expression becomes fiercely focused.
The samurai remains several meters behind her, momentarily surprised by her speed.
Suddenly, supernatural pink flames ignite around the woman's extended hand.
The flames rapidly spiral outward, forming a concentrated fire projectile.
Camera: Fast tracking movement with a brief slow-motion emphasis on her hair and the emerging flames.
VFX: Realistic fire illumination, glowing embers, heat distortion, and volumetric sparks.
SHOT 3 — THE FIRE PROJECTILE (0:06–0:09)
Over-the-shoulder cinematic shot from behind the woman.
She violently thrusts her arm forward.
A blazing pink fireball launches across the village street toward the samurai.
The projectile resembles a concentrated supernatural flame blast with a bright pink-white core and turbulent orange outer flames.
Its movement creates a powerful air-pressure disturbance.
Dust rises from the street.
Nearby wooden banners flutter violently.
The camera follows the projectile at high speed before cutting toward the samurai.
Sound: Explosive ignition, roaring flame, deep cinematic bass.
SHOT 4 — SAMURAI DEFENSE (0:09–0:11)
Dramatic medium close-up.
The samurai reacts with lightning-fast reflexes.
He draws his katana and raises it vertically in front of his face.
His expression becomes intensely focused.
The approaching fireball illuminates his face with flickering orange light.
His eyes narrow as he prepares to block the attack.
The flaming projectile approaches directly toward his raised sword.
Camera: Rapid push-in toward his eyes, followed by a close-up of the katana blade.
Sound: Metallic sword draw, rising fire roar, tension-building cinematic impact.
SHOT 5 — EXPLOSIVE IMPACT (0:11–0:13)
Extreme cinematic close-up.
The fireball collides with the samurai's defensive position.
A violent burst of orange flames, dark smoke, glowing sparks, and pressure waves erupts directly in front of his face.
The explosion briefly fills the frame.
The samurai's upper body recoils from the blast.
His dark kimono flutters from the shockwave.
Realistic soot and ash spread across his face.
Camera: Rapid impact shake with a brief slow-motion explosion.
Sound: Heavy explosive boom, roaring flames, crackling sparks.
SHOT 6 — THE HAIR EXPLOSION (0:13–0:16)
Extreme close-up of the samurai's face.
As the thick black smoke clears, reveal the unexpected result.
The samurai has survived the explosion, but his once-neat black hair has transformed into an enormous, wildly expanded, spiky hairstyle.
His hair sticks outward in every direction, resembling a massive electrified black cloud.
His face is covered in soot, dust, and tiny burn marks.
His eyes are wide open.
His mouth hangs slightly open in absolute disbelief.
Small wisps of smoke rise from his hair.
He remains completely frozen, staring forward in stunned silence.
Camera: Locked extreme close-up with a very slow dramatic push-in.
Sound: Sudden comedic silence, faint crackling electricity, a soft ringing sound.
SHOT 7 — THE HILARIOUS REACTION (0:16–0:20)
Medium-wide frontal shot.
The woman walks into frame beside the samurai.
Both characters stand in the middle of the village street.
The samurai remains frozen with his enormous exploded hairstyle.
The woman turns toward him, visibly shocked by his appearance.
Her eyes widen and her mouth opens slightly.
The samurai slowly raises one hand toward his head.
He touches the gigantic spiky hair, realizing what has happened.
His expression changes from confusion to complete embarrassment.
The woman looks at his hair again, struggling to process the absurd result.
End on a cinematic two-character reaction shot, emphasizing the contrast between her perfectly flowing hair and his ridiculous explosion-damaged hairstyle.
Sound: Light comedic sting, faint wind, subtle village ambience.
Hold the final reaction for maximum comedic effect.
CAMERA AND CINEMATOGRAPHY
Use premium live-action Japanese action-film cinematography.
Realistic handheld movement during combat.
Smooth tracking shots during character movement.
Dramatic low-angle framing during the fire attack.
Tight close-ups highlighting eyes and expressions.
Natural shallow depth of field.
Cinematic motion blur during fast movements.
Brief high-frame-rate slow motion during key action beats.
Dynamic exposure adjustments when fire illuminates the environment.
Realistic lens behavior without artificial camera distortion.
VISUAL EFFECTS
The supernatural fire must look physically convincing.
Flames should contain turbulent orange, yellow, and white layers.
Include realistic volumetric smoke, sparks, airborne embers, atmospheric heat distortion, and flickering firelight.
The explosion must interact naturally with the samurai's clothing, hair, and surrounding air.
The exaggerated hairstyle should appear physically attached to his head rather than resembling a wig or floating object.
Individual hair strands must remain visible.
SOUND DESIGN
Create a layered cinematic soundscape featuring:
Natural Japanese village ambience.
Fabric movement during martial arts.
Powerful kick whooshes.
Metallic katana unsheathing.
Supernatural flame ignition.
Roaring fire projectile.
Deep explosion impact.
Falling ash and crackling embers.
Sudden comedic silence.
Subtle humorous musical sting during the final reveal.
Music begins as intense Japanese action orchestration, combining taiko drums, deep percussion, dramatic strings, and rising tension.
Immediately after the explosion, the music cuts to silence before transitioning into a short playful comedic accent.
TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS
Duration: Exactly 20 seconds.
Aspect ratio: 16:9 widescreen.
Resolution: 4K.
Frame rate: 30 FPS, with simulated high-speed slow-motion moments.
Visual quality: Photorealistic, cinematic, detailed, high dynamic range.
Lighting: Natural daylight with physically accurate fire illumination.
Genre: Japanese fantasy martial arts action comedy.
Style: Premium live-action movie, not anime or cartoon.
STRICT CONSISTENCY REQUIREMENTS
Maintain identical character identities across every shot.
Preserve the woman's hairstyle, face, outfit, and body proportions.
Preserve the samurai's face, costume, and katana.
The samurai's hair must remain normal before the explosion and become dramatically enlarged and spiky only after the fire impact.
No random character transformations.
No extra limbs or fingers.
No distorted facial anatomy.
No unrealistic body movement.
No teleportation.
No inconsistent village architecture.
No disappearing weapons.
No abrupt costume changes.
No text, subtitles, or watermarks.
FINAL CREATIVE DIRECTION: The sequence must begin like a serious, intense Japanese fantasy martial arts film, build toward a spectacular supernatural fire attack, and end with an unexpected visual comedy payoff. The explosion must feel genuinely powerful, while the samurai's absurd hairstyle and stunned reaction provide the memorable final moment.
```

</details>

**再テスト:** Grok Imagine · 2026-10-03 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 54.8) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-16-9-widescreen-30-seconds-dola-continuous-engine-f6be640f9715/video-grok-imagine-20261003-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（24 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
16:9 widescreen, 30 seconds, Dola Continuous Engine.
STYLE LOCK: Inspired by classic late-1960s Irwin Allen television production "Land of the Giants". Authentic 1968 television aesthetic. Practical miniature sets. Optical compositing. Slight matte-line separation between actress and background. Rear-projection feel. Eastmancolor television colors: warm skin, saturated red and yellow, slightly cyan shadows. Soft image sharpness. Mild film grain. Analog broadcast texture. Vintage studio lighting: one hard key from upper left, warm fill from the right, no rim light. Camera: static wide shots and slow television zoom lens only. No handheld. No modern lens flare.
CHARACTER LOCK: @[Image 1](image_1)  is the only person. She remains identical in every frame:  1960s Korean mini dress  with [sleeve and neckline  classic 1960s makeup with winged liner and pale pink lips, white heels. Dress, hair, makeup and shoes never change .
SETTING LOCK: an enormous 1960s dining table seen from her scale; she is the size of a salt shaker. White tablecloth hanging from the edge to the floor in long vertical folds. On the tabletop from left to right: a tall glass of milk with condensation beads, a giant slice of chocolate layer cake with thick frosting and a single lit birthday candle, a large silver spoon and fork crossed beside it, a bowl of vanilla ice cream with one huge cherry on top, a folded white napkin. Warm wooden tabletop. Pale yellow wall in the background. Props are practical scale with slightly soft edges.
0–4 seconds:
Static wide from floor level. The tiny woman climbs the hanging tablecloth, gripping the folds with both hands, one heel finding a fold below her. The cloth sways slightly under her weight. She looks up toward the table edge.
4–7 seconds:
Slow zoom in as she pulls herself over the edge, kneels, then stands upright on the tabletop. Her eyes widen and her mouth opens, gaze drawn toward the flickering candle flame on the cake. Her hands stay half raised.
7–11 seconds:
Static wide from behind her at her eye level. The dessert spread stretches across the table like a city skyline: the cake slice looms like a frosted mountain, the candle flame taller than she is, the milk glass glowing like a white tower, the spoon lying across the foreground like a bridge.
11–15 seconds:
She walks toward the cake, heels tapping the tabletop. Thick ridges of frosting drip down its side. The lit candle flickers above, casting warm moving light across her face. She touches the frosting edge with one hand and looks up along the flame.
15–19 seconds:
She climbs onto the cake's bottom layer, sits, scoops a bit of frosting with both hands, smiles, tastes it, licks her fingers, and looks left and right. Medium shot, slow zoom in to her face, candlelight flickering warmly on her skin.
19–23 seconds:
She stands and walks farther across the tabletop, stepping around the giant cherry from the ice cream bowl, passing between the spoon and the napkin. Static wide, then a slow pan following her.
23–26 seconds:
She stops beside the milk glass, looks up along the tall glass, and laughs with one hand resting on it. Condensation beads glisten and catch the light on her face.
26–28 seconds:
A massive shadow sweeps across the tabletop from the right and covers the milk glass and the cake. Her laugh stops. She lowers her hand and turns to face the right side of frame.
28–30 seconds:
HOLD. Camera fully static medium-wide shot. She stands facing screen right, feet together, right hand half raised at chest height, left hand at her side. The milk glass directly at her left, the cake slice behind her, the shadow across the whole tabletop. Her face is turned to the right, not yet tilted upward. Nothing moves for two full seconds.
Audio
Cheerful 1960s adventure television music, playful brass and strings. At 26 seconds the music thins to one held string note that continues to the end, with a faint fading birthday-music-box
```

```
右边是原视频，喜欢拿去做动态壁纸
不只好玩，其实还能做成 AI 视频小副业！

Seedance 2.5制作    
Prompt🔽

参考上传图片中的同一位成年女性角色，保持脸型、五官、浅金色长发、空气刘海、深蓝色花朵发夹、妆容与整体造型一致。

生成一段 8 秒、9:16 竖屏、固定机位、一镜到底的正面超近景人物视频。

整体表演：软萌、调皮、可爱、娇俏，带一点自然撒娇和假装委屈感。
重点表现眼神、嘴唇、眉毛、脸颊和轻微头部跟随产生的真实微表情。
0–1.5 秒

女主正视镜头，神态柔和可爱，嘴唇自然微张后轻轻合拢。
伴随呼吸，头部和下巴产生极小幅自然移动。

1.5–3.7 秒
眼睛先慢慢向画面左侧移动，偷偷侧瞄旁边。
头部稍晚才向同方向轻微跟随，移动幅度明显小于眼神，形成调皮、偷偷观察的感觉。

3.7–4.6 秒

自然完成一次双眼眨眼。
视线重新回到镜头，头部同时轻轻回正，恢复软萌自然的表情。
4.6–5.2 秒

眼睛先向下看，下巴随后轻微降低。
一只手从画面右下方自然进入，食指缓慢靠近画面右侧脸颊。
女主观察手指，嘴唇开始轻轻向前收拢。

5.2–6.6 秒
食指轻轻按压脸颊，脸颊产生真实柔软的轻微凹陷。
接触瞬间女主双眼短暂眯起，头部顺着触碰产生极轻微的受力让位和侧倾。

随后重新睁眼看向镜头：

眉头轻轻收拢 + 下唇微微鼓起 + 嘴角轻微向下
形成调皮可爱、撒娇式的假装委屈表情。
6.6–8 秒

食指离开脸颊，脸颊自然回弹，头部轻轻回正。

委屈表情连续释放：
嘴唇放松 → 眉头舒展 → 自然快速眨一次双眼。

最后重新正视镜头，嘴唇轻轻微张，恢复甜甜、软萌、略带调皮感的自然表情。
```

```
00:23–00:27 — THE DETAIL

00:27–00:30 — FINAL IMAGE
```

```
Create an ultra-realistic early-2000s Korean MiniDV vlog featuring a Korean woman in her early 20s visiting a tiny neighborhood nail salon in 2004. She has shoulder-length dark brown hair, natural makeup, a pastel cardigan, and a knee-length skirt.

Shot 1 (0–3s): Handheld footage follows her entering the small salon, sitting at a manicure table, browsing vintage nail polish bottles, and choosing a soft-pink shade.

Shot 2 (3–5s): Close-up of her selecting the polish bottle while the technician prepares the manicure. She casually flips through an old fashion magazine.

Shot 3 (5–8s): Extreme close-up of the technician carefully filing and shaping her natural, unpainted fingernails.

Shot 4 (8–12s): Macro shots of glossy soft-pink polish being applied evenly to each nail. She watches with curiosity and slightly wiggles her fingers.

Shot 5 (12–15s): She raises both hands toward the camcorder, shows off her finished glossy nails, turns her wrists to catch the light, and smiles cheerfully.

Visual & Audio Style: Authentic 2004 South Korean neighborhood salon, consumer MiniDV footage with 4:3 source-video characteristics inside a 16:9 frame. Handheld shake, imperfect autofocus, soft tape detail, faded colors, warm fluorescent lighting, and natural exposure fluctuations. Include realistic filing sounds, polish bottle clicks, magazine pages, quiet conversation, and faint street ambience. Avoid polished commercial-style cinematography.

Continuity: Maintain identical character appearance, clothing, salon, technician, and nail polish shade throughout. Show natural progression from unpainted to filed to freshly painted nails. Only two anatomically correct hands with five fingers each.

Overall Feeling: An ordinary, charming afternoon in South Korea in 2004, captured as a nostalgic personal home-video memory.
```

```
Ultra-realistic live-action Korean revenge-comedy along Seoul’s Han River at golden hour. Handheld cinematic camera, warm sunset, authentic park ambience, realistic physics, fast-paced slapstick action, expressive reactions.

**Characters:** Lee Ji-won Korean woman in her 20s, high ponytail, mint-green windbreaker, black cycling shorts, sneakers, riding a city bicycle. Shin Tae-oh — Korean man in his 20s, black cap, grey hoodie, riding an electric scooter. Korean grandmother wearing a purple visor.

**30-SECOND ACTION:**
0–3s: Grandmother sits on a bench with her brown handbag. Tae-oh snatches it and speeds away on his scooter.
3–6s: She cries out. Ji-won hears her, shouts “저기요!”, and starts chasing on her bicycle.
6–12s: Tae-oh weaves through joggers, laughing. Ji-won catches up, follows him across grass, and launches off a small ramp, landing with a hard bounce.
12–18s: Tae-oh scatters a family's picnic snacks. Ji-won hops over the blanket, shouting “거기 서!”, then grabs the handbag strap beside his scooter. A brief, chaotic tug-of-war makes the scooter wobble.
18–25s: Ji-won yanks the bag free. Tae-oh loses control, spins off the path, flies over the handlebars, and crashes into a large circular fountain with a huge splash. Pigeons scatter.
25–30s: Ji-won returns the bag to the grandmother and asks, “다 괜찮으세요, 할머니?” The grandmother smiles and pats her hand. Behind them, the drenched Tae-oh sits in the fountain beside his floating cap as amused joggers and families clap and film.

**Audio:** Natural Korean-only dialogue, perfectly lip-synced, wind, bicycle chains, scooter motor, footsteps, startled gasps, water splash, pigeons, traffic, and applause.

**Requirements:** Consistent character identities, faces, outfits, and location throughout. Clearly show the theft, ramp jump, picnic leap, tug-of-war, scooter spin-out, and fountain crash. Believable momentum and water physics. Photorealistic skin, natural movement, non-gory slapstick. No logos, brands, subtitles, text overlays, cartoon effects, or watermark.
```

### Woman Battles Ten Masked Fighters in a Rain-Soaked Tea Garden

> made a fight scene on a misty mountain and honestly the pole-vault double kick got me 😭 Created with Seedance 2.5 on @FishCreativeHQ Prompt: 16:9 widescreen, c…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/f3cd346b08c6.jpg" width="600" alt="Woman Battles Ten Masked Fighters in a Rain-Soaked Tea Garden">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-16-9-widescreen-cinematic-live-action-15-seconds-high-momentum-parkour-fight-41e698499a8d?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Chaemate_ | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Chaemate_/status/2113558308514922850) | **公開日:** 2026-10-23 | **ヒート:** 50

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（30 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
16:9 widescreen, cinematic live-action, 15 seconds, high-momentum parkour fight in a continuous-feeling sequence. Hard cuts only between shots, no dissolves, morphs or transitions; motion and direction match across every cut. No dialogue; only natural footsteps, fabric, splashes and impact sounds.

PROTAGONIST: @image 1 (facial identity, hair, rose-pink streaks, long side braid, plum shirt and cream cargo trousers locked to the reference throughout). Her movement is sharp, decisive and fluid. She dominates the entire fight and takes down ten masked attackers in a rapid chain.

OPPONENTS: Ten masked "Ashen Guard" fighters in charcoal hooded rain coats and slate face wraps, armed with bamboo staffs and short batons. They close in from above, from the sides and from behind. Every one is hit cleanly and reacts visibly: knocked back, off-balance, or thrown into a wall, rack or railing before leaving frame. The next attacker steps in immediately.

SETTING: An abandoned terraced tea plantation on a steep misty mountainside just after rain. Mossy stone terrace walls, narrow wet stone staircases, a shallow water-filled irrigation channel running down the slope, a collapsed wooden footbridge with a gap, rusted tea-drying racks, leaning bamboo scaffolding and a thin waterfall at the cliff edge. Low golden sun cuts in from the side through light haze. Palette: moss green, wet slate grey, warm amber, deep plum. Kicked-up water spray, mud flecks, torn tea leaves and mist react to every impact. Realistic natural lighting only.

SHOT 1 (0-2.5s) Low-angle, ground level, FOV 35 degrees. She bursts up from the bottom of the stone terrace, launches off a step and drives a flying front kick into attacker 1's chest, throwing him back into a stone wall. She lands and slides sideways under attacker 2's staff swing, then snaps a rising elbow to his chin; he drops flat into the wet tea bushes.

SHOT 2 (2.5-5s) Tracking from the side, FOV 40 degrees. She plants her foot on the mossy terrace wall, runs three steps up it and delivers a diagonal kick to attacker 3, who loses balance and tumbles down the staircase. Without pause she uses a leaning bamboo pole to spring into the air and drives a knee into attacker 4, who collapses onto the stones.

SHOT 3 (5-7.5s) Camera glides close to the ground and sweeps upward, FOV 30 degrees. She sprints downhill in a low crouch under hanging bamboo and ropes, spins into a sweeping kick that sends attacker 5 crashing into a rusted tea rack. Hand-plant roll, then a flying kick into attacker 6, who skids backward into the edge of the terrace wall.

SHOT 4 (7.5-10s) Camera orbits her, FOV 32 degrees. She slides on her knees through the shallow irrigation channel, throwing up a wide sheet of water, and pops up at the collapsed footbridge. She vaults the gap and twists mid-air to ram her shoulder into attacker 7, sending him to the terrace below. She lands, catches a bamboo pole thrown by attacker 8, plants it and pole-vaults, driving both feet into attackers 8 and 9, who fly in opposite directions.

SHOT 5 (10-12.5s) Camera pushes forward as if chasing, then pulls back fast, FOV 38 degrees. She runs along the leaning bamboo scaffolding, then back-flips over attacker 10's sneak attack from behind. Mid-air she twists and lands a back kick between his shoulder blades, sending him rolling forward down the slope.

SHOT 6 (12.5-15s) Camera drops to a low wide angle, FOV 30 degrees, then locks front-on at eye level. She lands and slides through a puddle in a low crouch, water spraying around her, then rises into a sharp ready stance. Her braid and the hem of her shirt are still swinging from the speed. The sequence ends sharply on this strong, still pose, final frame in crisp focus.

CAMERA: Momentum-driven tracking that follows her center of gravity (jumps, wall-runs, pole vault, kicks) rather than sitting static. Reactive impact cinematography: a sharp small shake on every hit, the camera briefly following a thrown opponent, then snapping back to her. Natural motion blur on fast moves.

PACING: Speed builds through the explosive moments. Her actions are clear and decisive; enemy reactions are fast and heavy. No slow motion, no pauses, no posed moments, no wasted movement.

VISUAL QUALITY: Ultra-high definition, cinema grade, realistic skin, fabric and water physics, live-action photographic look.

POSITIVE LOCKS: Pink streaks stay a soft rose tone, braid stays on her right shoulder, plum shirt and cream cargo trousers unchanged, boots and gloves unchanged, exactly ten attackers in charcoal coats and slate face wraps.

CONSTRAINTS: At least ten enemies, all hit. No empty kicks; every strike connects. No repeated moves. No fantasy light effects, no neon colors, no neon glow, no cartoon or CGI-style look, no distorted limbs, no clipping or objects passing through each other, no stuttering motion, no extra people, no text or watermarks.
#AIvideo #AIart #Seedance #AIFilmmaking #Parkour
```

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（52 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Created a 30s woodland fantasy scene straight out of a forgotten 90s fantasy movie.#wizstarPartner

Seedance 2.5 x @WizstarAI

Try it here :
https://t.co/lP61ezavMd

Prompt 👇🏻

Create an original young adult female woodland guardian in the handcrafted style of a forgotten early-1990s live-action fantasy film. Fully original character and world, practical-effects aesthetic, not modern CGI.

CHARACTER: Young adult woman, natural expressive face, amber-brown eyes, softly angular features, subtly elongated fantasy ears, thick dark auburn wavy hair with small braids. Intelligent, brave, curious and quietly playful.

COSTUME: Fully modest sage-green high-neck linen blouse, brown leather vest, ankle-length olive wool skirt, opaque dark leggings, worn brown boots, leather wrist guards and long burnt-orange hooded cloak. Leather belt with small pouches, brass compass, herbs, wooden whistle and bronze leaf pendant. Real fibers, worn leather, tarnished metal and natural mud. Practical prosthetic fantasy ears.

SETTING: Ancient woodland at dusk, twisted trees, moss, ferns, mushrooms, streams, wooden bridges, carved stones and handmade lanterns. Hidden shelters and distant lights suggest a secret woodland settlement.

STYLE: Practical foliage, physical props, theatrical mist, practical lanterns, miniature sets, restrained optical effects, 35mm film softness, subtle grain, lantern halation, earthy greens, browns and amber, warm theatrical lighting. No glossy CGI or modern fantasy aesthetic.

[00:00-00:04] THE PATH
Low shot of her boots walking through damp leaves. Camera rises to her face. A distant metallic chime makes her stop.

[00:04-00:08] THE LANTERN
She finds a glowing antique lantern with no visible flame. It goes dark as she reaches for it. Another lantern lights deeper in the forest, then another. She realizes she is being guided.

[00:08-00:12] THE TRAIL
She follows the lanterns and discovers carved symbols on trees. One rotates when touched. Another moves behind her. Nobody is there. She smiles, realizing someone is watching.

[00:12-00:16] THE FOX
A woodland fox leads her to a fallen tree. Beneath its roots she finds a box containing a brass compass. Its spinning needle suddenly points upward. A shadow moves through the canopy.

[00:16-00:20] SOMEONE ABOVE
Leaves rustle. Acorns drop beside her boots. She looks upward and says softly, "You could simply come down." A hidden wooden platform creaks above. She smiles suspiciously.

[00:20-00:24] THE DOOR
She follows the sound to an enormous tree and discovers a hidden seam. She touches it. Carved symbols glow and the bark opens into a secret doorway filled with warm golden light. She enters.

[00:24-00:27] THE HIDDEN WORLD
Inside is a vast woodland settlement built around the tree: lanterns, wooden platforms, rope bridges, workshops, herbs and glowing windows. Hundreds of lanterns illuminate as she looks around in wonder.

[00:27-00:30] THE INVITATION
A wooden bridge leads to a folded parchment reading "YOU FOUND US." Several silhouettes appear among the lanterns. She smiles, places the parchment in her satchel and walks deeper into the settlement.

Camera pulls backward through the ancient tree doorway into the dark forest. Cut to black.

STRICT CONTINUITY: Same face, hair, ears, proportions, costume, cloak, boots, belt, pendant and satchel in every shot. Fully modest clothing with no wardrobe changes or exposure. Continuous geography and logical progression.

CAMERA: Atmospheric low angles, slow tracking, facial close-ups, wide environmental shots and restrained handheld movement.

FINAL LOOK: Authentic early-1990s 35mm fantasy film, practical prosthetics, real costumes, physical sets, theatrical mist, practical lanterns, subtle optical effects, natural performances and beautiful film grain.

Original character and world only. No recognizable existing characters, franchises, logos, branded costumes, copied designs, blood or gore.
```

</details>

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/9377bc7b94f2.jpg" width="600" alt="Female Surgeon's Hospital Rescue Mission">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-okay-this-feels-straight-out-of-gta-4dcfe15565a6?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**再テスト:** Grok Imagine · 2026-10-03 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 72.8) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-okay-this-feels-straight-out-of-gta-4dcfe15565a6/video-grok-imagine-20261003-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（48 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
Okay this feels straight out of GTA
 
Seedance 2.5 on @itsPolloAI 

Prompt

Create a 30-second ultra-realistic AAA third-person medical action game mission set inside a modern Japanese hospital, with the feel of premium GTA-style gameplay.
NAGI: Use the exact same NAGI identity and face from @ Image1 throughout. Young Japanese female surgeon with long straight black hair, blunt bangs, blue surgical scrubs, cap, mask, gloves, and hospital shoes. Perfect character consistency.

GAMEPLAY: One continuous playable third-person over-the-shoulder take from 0–27 seconds. Natural running, turning, grabbing equipment, opening doors, interacting with machines, and performing procedures. No montage or random cuts. One hard cut only at 27 seconds.

HUD throughout:
TOP LEFT: NAGI / DOCTOR LV. 12
TOP CENTER: MISSION: SAVE THE PATIENT
BOTTOM RIGHT: HEALTH: 100% / TIME: 03:42

0–4s — CHECK VITALS
NAGI rushes into the emergency room, checks the unconscious patient's unstable monitor, and connects the monitoring equipment.
HUD: OBJECTIVE 1/5 — CHECK PATIENT VITALS → OBJECTIVE COMPLETE

4–8s — STABILIZE
She connects an IV and adjusts the patient's oxygen support. The monitor becomes slightly more stable.
HUD: OBJECTIVE 2/5 — STABILIZE PATIENT → OBJECTIVE COMPLETE

8–12s — COLLECT TOOLS
NAGI runs to the preparation area, opens a medical cabinet, grabs a sterile surgical tray, receives another instrument from a nurse, and rushes back.
HUD: OBJECTIVE 3/5 — COLLECT SURGICAL TOOLS → OBJECTIVE COMPLETE

12–17s — SURGERY
She enters the operating room, positions herself beside the patient, surgical lights activate, and she performs the critical procedure with controlled, non-graphic movements.
HUD: OBJECTIVE 4/5 — COMPLETE CRITICAL PROCEDURE

17–22s — COMPLICATION
The monitor suddenly alarms and vital signs drop. NAGI immediately checks the monitor, adjusts support equipment, signals the team, and continues the procedure.
HUD: WARNING — PATIENT UNSTABLE → STABILIZE PATIENT

22–27s — SAVE PATIENT
NAGI completes the final step. The monitor becomes steady, the alarm stops, and the team relaxes.
HUD: OBJECTIVE 5/5 COMPLETE → PATIENT STABLE → SURGERY SUCCESSFUL → MISSION COMPLETE

27–30s — FINAL SHOT
One hard cut to a locked frontal gameplay-style shot outside the operating room. NAGI walks out exhausted but relieved and removes her mask while a nurse follows. Through the glass, the team monitors the patient.

Final HUD: MISSION COMPLETE / PATIENT SAVED / RANK: S

VISUAL STYLE: Photorealistic AAA game-engine graphics, realistic Japanese hospital, detailed medical equipment, natural character physics, realistic lighting, reflections, shadows, cloth and glove movement, subtle camera shake. No anime, cartoon, fantasy, superhero effects, or gore.

AUDIO: Hospital ambience, footsteps, monitor beeps, equipment sounds, emergency alarm, Japanese nurse/NAGI dialogue, and tense AAA game music building toward the mission completion.
```

</details>

```
EXACT 30 SECONDS

Aspect Ratio: 16:9

Create an ultra-photorealistic live-action cinematic travel vlog featuring the exact male identity from @image1 exploring New York City, USA. Preserve his exact facial identity, hairstyle, beard, skin tone and body proportions. Maintain the same olive-green travel jacket, white T-shirt, black cargo trousers, white sneakers, black backpack and wristwatch in every shot. Use the facial close-up panels of @image1 for identity consistency and the headless full-body panels for wardrobe and body proportions.

Create a professional 30-second travel documentary with 10 visually distinct shots, realistic human performance, cinematic multi-angle cinematography and smooth narrative continuity.

00:00–00:03 — Times Square: Medium-wide 28mm handheld backward tracking shot. The vlogger smiles and gestures toward enormous digital billboards while yellow taxis and pedestrians move naturally behind him.

00:03–00:06 — Brooklyn Bridge: Wide 24mm stabilized backward tracking shot as he walks along the pedestrian walkway with historic stone towers, suspension cables and Manhattan skyline.

00:06–00:09 — Statue of Liberty: 50mm cinematic push-in at a New York Harbor viewpoint. The vlogger points toward the monument while realistic ferries and water reflections appear in the background.

00:09–00:12 — Manhattan Streets: Dynamic 35mm lateral tracking shot. A yellow taxi passes naturally as the vlogger walks and speaks to camera.

00:12–00:15 — Central Park: Smooth 35mm Steadicam push-in as he explores tree-lined pathways with green landscapes and distant skyscrapers.

00:15–00:18 — Vlogger Close-Up: Intimate 85mm close-up with sharp eye focus, realistic facial expressions, natural dialogue and soft green background.

00:18–00:21 — Empire State Building: Dramatic 24mm low-angle upward tilt revealing the iconic skyscraper as the vlogger looks upward and gestures toward it.

00:21–00:24 — Midtown Manhattan: 50mm smooth backward tracking shot as he walks through towering skyscrapers while speaking directly to the audience.

00:24–00:27 — Brooklyn Waterfront: Spectacular 35mm semicircular 180-degree orbit revealing the Manhattan skyline across the East River during golden hour.

00:27–00:30 — Final Closing: 40mm slow cinematic dolly-out as the vlogger walks beside the waterfront, turns toward the camera and smiles with the glowing Manhattan skyline behind him. Fade to black exactly at 30 seconds.

Cinematography: Professional cinema-camera aesthetics, realistic lens characteristics, natural depth of field, smooth physically achievable camera movement, cinematic composition, authentic motion blur and subtle film grain.

Lighting: Natural daylight transitioning subtly into golden-hour warmth. Realistic shadows, physically accurate reflections and consistent environmental lighting.

Audio: Original upbeat cinematic travel music combining acoustic guitar, electronic percussion, bass and orchestral strings. Gradually build the score, soften it during dialogue, reach an emotional peak during the skyline reveal and finish with a warm orchestral chord. Include authentic New York traffic, yellow taxi engines, horns, pedestrians, footsteps, harbor breeze and river ambience. Natural synchronized dialogue with clear voice priority.

Performance: Natural walking, realistic hand gestures, authentic facial expressions, subtle eye movement, confident travel-vlogger personality and believable interaction with the environment.

Continuity: Same face, beard, hairstyle, clothing, backpack, accessories and body proportions in all 10 shots. Maintain realistic geography, consistent lighting and seamless visual progression. No random locations or unnecessary characters.
```

```
Create a hyper-realistic 15-second Hollywood action sequence inside a gritty retro-American industrial tavern. 16:9 widescreen, 4K, 60fps, HDR10+, authentic 35mm Kodak film texture, realistic lighting and intense close-quarters combat.

CHARACTER REFERENCES:
@image1 = Male protagonist. Preserve exact face, beard, hairstyle, skin tone and body proportions. Outfit: crisp white formal shirt, dark-gray pinstriped waistcoat, matching trousers, black bow tie, black fingerless gloves and matte-black formal leather shoes. No identity or wardrobe changes.

@image2 = Female boss antagonist. Preserve her exact face, dark braided hair, charcoal-black combat suit, dark-red leather corset armor, torn shoulder sash, metal greaves and black tactical boots. She carries exactly one standard-sized Nepalese kukri.

@image3 = Fixed tavern environment. Maintain dark wooden bar, leather booths, industrial metal supports, wooden tables, chairs, pendant lights, dusty windows and spacious combat floor.

ACTION TIMELINE:

00:00–00:03: Male protagonist bursts into the tavern with an explosive spinning flying kick, knocking the first machete thug into a booth. Immediately performs two rapid leg-sweep and grappling takedowns against approaching attackers.

00:03–00:06: Two steel-pipe attackers approach simultaneously. He performs an aerial flying scissor-takedown, throwing them in opposite directions into wooden furniture. Immediately blocks another pipe attack and throws the attacker against the bar.

00:06–00:09: He ducks a machete strike, executes a powerful shoulder throw, evades the final steel-pipe attacker and topples him. The female boss suddenly emerges from the shadows, charging with her kukri.

00:09–00:12: The boss launches a powerful overhead kukri attack. The protagonist narrowly dodges backward. The blade embeds into the wooden bar, splitting timber. He immediately grabs her legs and initiates a powerful 360-degree rotational throw.

00:12–00:15: He releases the boss, sending her crashing into the thick wooden wall. The wall splinters violently. Camera tracks the impact before cutting to a dramatic close-up of the protagonist facing the remaining threat.

CAMERA & PHYSICS: Dynamic low-angle tracking, fast handheld movement, cinematic wide shots and sharp close-ups. Realistic stunt choreography, believable inertia, solid physical impacts and practical destruction.

STRICT RULES: Maximum two enemies attack simultaneously. No phantom falls, clipping, overlapping bodies, teleportation, slow motion, floating movements or artificial effects. Preserve all character identities, clothing, weapons and environment. Every fall must result from visible physical contact.

AUDIO: Original aggressive Hollywood action music, synchronized punches, metallic clashes, wood breaking, footsteps and heavy impact sounds. No dialogue, subtitles or watermarks.

Exactly 15 seconds. One continuous, relentless, photorealistic cinematic action sequence.
```

</details>

```
Sound Design: Heavy footsteps, realistic punch and kick impacts, rushing wind, stone debris, clothing movement, deep cinematic impacts, subtle epic orchestral background music.

Consistency Requirements: Maintain the same female character, facial features, hairstyle, black outfit, male opponents, red uniforms, and white quarry environment throughout the entire video. Natural anatomy, realistic balance, continuous motion, coherent fight choreography, no duplicated limbs, no distorted faces, no sudden scene changes, no text, no subtitles, no logos.

Duration: 15 seconds. Aspect ratio: 16:9. Quality: Ultra-realistic cinematic 4K.
```

<summary><b>プロンプト全文（181 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
30 SECONDS | 9:16 | 4K | 60 FPS
ULTRA-PHOTOREALISTIC JAPANESE ACTION CINEMA
COMBAT SAMBO × DIRTY BOXING × SAVATE
6 SHOTS / EXACTLY 5 CUTS
ONE 0.25S MICRO SLOW-MOTION MOMENT

01 — ABSOLUTE CHARACTER & ENEMY LOCK

@Image 1 — BLACK ROSE:
Preserve exact reference face, pale natural skin, messy black updo, black fitted clothing, black floral embroidered outerwear, necklaces and heeled lace-up boots. Cold, confident, rebellious attitude. No visual changes.

VICTIM:
Adult Japanese female street-food vendor, approximately 30, shoulder-length dark hair, cream blouse, dark-green apron.

EXACTLY THREE ATTACKERS:

A — Tall man in a black leather jacket.
B — Broad-shouldered man in a dark-red bomber jacket.
C — Lean man in a beige utility jacket.

No additional attackers, duplicate people or replacement characters.

02 — FIXED GEOGRAPHY & LOCATION

Location: Narrow Tokyo night-market alley after rainfall.

- SCREEN-LEFT: Small ramen stall with a wooden counter and hanging red lantern.
- CENTER: Female vendor standing behind the counter.
- SCREEN-RIGHT: Three attackers standing in the pedestrian alley.
- BACKGROUND: Neon signs, closed storefronts, wet pavement and distant market lights.

Keep the same camera axis and screen positions in every shot.

03 — SIX-SHOT ACTION SEQUENCE

SHOT 1 | 0–5S — THE STALL IS DESTROYED

CAMERA: 35mm wide establishing tracking shot.

A grabs the wooden counter and knocks several empty bowls onto the ground.

The vendor steps backward, frightened.

VICTIM:
「やめてください！」
Please stop!

B kicks a small empty stool aside. C stands beside A, blocking the vendor's exit.

At the far end of the alley, @Image 1 walks into view carrying a small black handbag.

CUT 1 — ON THE SOUND OF A BOWL HITTING THE GROUND.

SHOT 2 | 5–10S — BLACK ROSE ARRIVES

CAMERA: 40mm low-angle forward tracking shot.

@Image 1 stops beside the overturned stool.

She looks at the vendor, then slowly turns toward A.

BLACK ROSE:
「店から離れな。」
Get away from her stall.

A laughs and steps toward her.

A:
「どけよ。」
Move.

He throws a committed right punch.

@Image 1 slips outside his attack and redirects his arm with a sharp defensive movement, making him stumble toward SCREEN-RIGHT.

CUT 2 — ON B STEPPING FORWARD.

SHOT 3 | 10–15S — THE HEAVY ATTACK

CAMERA: 35mm lateral tracking shot.

B rushes forward with a powerful shoulder-led charge.

@Image 1 sidesteps his momentum, delivers a compact body strike and follows with a controlled Savate push kick.

B loses balance and falls onto the wet pavement away from the stall.

B IS DOWN — remains down.

C moves forward immediately.

CUT 3 — ON C REACHING FOR HER WRIST.

SHOT 4 | 15–20S — THE GRIP BREAK

CAMERA: 50mm medium three-quarter shot.

C grabs toward her wrist.

@Image 1 rotates her arm free, steps to the outside and delivers a short elbow strike to his upper torso.

C staggers backward and falls beside a closed storefront.

C IS DOWN — remains down.

A regains his footing and rushes forward.

CUT 4 — ON A'S APPROACH.

SHOT 5 | 20–25S — THE FINAL COUNTER

CAMERA: 35mm low frontal action angle.

A launches a desperate swinging punch.

@Image 1 ducks, pivots on her supporting foot and delivers one explosive spinning back kick to his torso.

ONE 0.25-SECOND MICRO SLOW-MOTION AT CONTACT.

His jacket compresses and his upper body moves backward in the direction of the kick.

INSTANT RETURN TO REAL-TIME.

A falls onto the open wet pavement.

A IS DOWN — remains down.

CUT 5 — ON BLACK ROSE TURNING BACK TOWARD THE STALL.

SHOT 6 | 25–30S — THE PROTECTOR

CAMERA: 35mm slow reverse dolly.

@Image 1 walks toward the vendor and gently picks up a fallen bowl from the ground.

The vendor looks at her with relief.

VICTIM:
「本当にありがとうございます。」
Thank you so much.

BLACK ROSE places the bowl on the counter.

BLACK ROSE:
「もう大丈夫。」
It's okay now.

She turns and walks down the neon-lit alley.

Exactly three attackers remain down in the background.

04 — ABSOLUTE CONTINUITY RULES

ENEMY LEDGER:
A: Stumbles in Shot 2, falls in Shot 5.
B: Falls in Shot 3.
C: Falls in Shot 4.

FINAL COUNT: EXACTLY THREE FALLEN ATTACKERS.

POSITION LOCK:
B lies in the center-right wet alley.
C lies near the right-side closed storefront.
A lies farther down the right-side pedestrian lane.

The overturned stool and fallen bowls remain in their original positions unless physically picked up.

CAMERA AXIS:
Never cross the 180-degree axis. Maintain the stall on SCREEN-LEFT and attackers on SCREEN-RIGHT.

CONTACT PHYSICS:
Every strike shows contact, force transfer, recoil and correct fall direction. No teleportation, floating bodies or premature knockdowns.

WORLD REACTIONS:
Lanterns remain hanging, neon lighting stays consistent, and no additional people enter the foreground.

AUDIO:
Natural Japanese dialogue, rainwater dripping, ceramic bowls hitting pavement, footsteps, fabric movement and realistic non-graphic combat impacts. No background music.

NEGATIVE:
Extra attackers, cloned people, reversed geography, changing stall design, disappearing bowls, random weapons, blood, gore, excessive slow motion, distorted limbs, subtitles, text overlays, cartoon physics or abrupt cuts.
```

```
Create a premium, ultra-realistic Korean romantic mystery K-drama scene set during a family dinner in a modern Seoul apartment.

CHARACTER CONTINUITY:

- Han Seo-yeon (26): Long wavy dark-brown hair, cream blouse, black midi skirt, delicate earrings.
- Kang Min-jun (28): Neat black hair, charcoal shirt, black trousers.
- Mother: Late 50s, short dark hair, burgundy blouse.
- Father: Early 60s, greying hair, navy sweater.
- Sister: 24, long black hair, beige cardigan.

Maintain identical faces, clothing, hairstyles, and appearances throughout.

12-SHOT STORY:

1. Wide shot: Family dinner around a table filled with traditional Korean dishes. Seo-yeon sits nervously beside Min-jun.
2. Mother asks: "두 사람은 언제 헤어졌니?" (When did you two break up?)
3. Min-jun calmly introduces her: "제 전 여자친구예요." (She's my ex-girlfriend.) Seo-yeon freezes.
4. Sister looks uncomfortable; father keeps eating; mother nods knowingly.
5. Seo-yeon whispers: "지금 뭐라고 했어?" Min-jun replies: "제발 그냥 맞춰 줘. 나중에 설명할게." (Please, just play along. I'll explain later.)
6. Seo-yeon forces a smile and greets his mother: "오랜만에 뵙겠습니다."
7. Sister asks: "언니, 이번에도 오빠랑 다시 만나는 거예요?" (Are you getting back together again?)
8. Mother places an old framed photograph on the table showing Min-jun and Seo-yeon together.
9. Seo-yeon examines it and asks: "이 사진… 언제 찍은 거예요?" Min-jun looks down silently.
10. Mother asks: "이번에는 기억 못 하는 척하는 거니?" (Are you pretending not to remember this time?)
11. Terrified, Seo-yeon asks Min-jun: "우리 전에 만난 적 있어?" (Have we met before?) He says nothing.
12. Extreme close-up of the photograph's back, revealing handwritten Korean: "우리 결혼식 날." (Our wedding day.) Cut to black.

CINEMATOGRAPHY & AUDIO:
Premium K-drama cinematography, natural Korean dialogue and accurate lip-sync, realistic facial expressions, cinematic close-ups, subtle push-ins, warm apartment lighting, authentic dinner ambience, and soft piano gradually turning into suspenseful ambient music.

Maintain consistent character appearances, dining-table positions, photograph continuity, and natural performances.

ENDING: Leave the audience questioning whether Seo-yeon has lost her memory or whether Min-jun and his family are hiding a disturbing secret.
```

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/f2027a8ab748.jpg" width="600" alt="Dance Duo Across a Sunset Studio, Neon Alley and Tokyo Rooftop">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-15-second-cinematic-16-9-ultra-realistic-live-action-tokyo-street-style-dance-v-2f821bb72e06?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Chaemate_ | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/Chaemate_/status/2107691578605322348) | **公開日:** 2026-10-07 | **ヒート:** 48

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（62 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
15-second cinematic 16:9 ultra-realistic live-action Tokyo street-style dance video.

TWO JAPANESE FEMALE DANCERS ONLY.

IDENTITY LOCK:
Preserve @image1 exact face, facial structure, eyes, nose, lips, eyebrows, hairstyle and identity.
Preserve @image2 exact face, facial structure, eyes, nose, lips, eyebrows, hairstyle and identity.
Keep their exact reference outfits and accessories in every scene. Natural human proportions. Do not alter either face at any moment.

MUSIC (drives all the action):
Upbeat Japanese city-pop x dance-pop track, 120 BPM (one beat every 0.5s). Funky slap bass, bright synth stabs, snappy claps, light female vocal hook. Structure: intro groove (0–5s), bass-heavy main drop (5–10s), breakdown with a final hit (10–15s). Every movement lands exactly on the beat, and the energy of the choreography follows the music.

DANCE STYLE:
Playful, cool and confident, a mix of commercial hip-hop, city-pop groove and K-pop performance energy. Teasing eye contact, small smirks, shoulder accents. Stylish and controlled, never exaggerated. Sync moments where both dance together, and mirror moments where they play off each other.

SCENE 1 (0–5s): Minimalist dance studio at golden hour
Grey concrete wall, polished floor with soft reflections, a large window showing the Tokyo skyline at sunset. Warm light spilling across the floor.
0.0–1.0s: Both walk into frame side by side, snap to a stop on the first bass note, quick shoulder hit.
1.0–2.0s: Sharp right-arm sweep across the chest, step sideways, then left arm snaps outward with a hip accent.
2.0–3.0s: Chest isolation into shoulder roll, and one dancer playfully touches her hair while the other points at the camera.
3.0–4.0s: Two-step footwork with alternating arm punches, then arms cross and rapidly open on the synth stab.
4.0–5.0s: Synchronized body lean right then left, with one arm sweeping over the head. On the final beat they spin and whip-pan toward the window.

TRANSITION: Fast whip-pan with natural motion blur, matched to the beat. No glitch or CGI effects.

SCENE 2 (5–10s): Neon alley in Shibuya at night
Wet pavement reflecting pink and blue neon signs, paper lanterns, light drizzle mist, a few blurred pedestrians far in the background.
5.0–6.0s: The bass drops. Both step toward the camera with a confident synchronized arm gesture.
6.0–7.0s: Low hip-hop bounce with bent knees, two shoulder hits, then a controlled body roll and a sharp hand flick.
7.0–8.0s: Dancers split: one slides left, the other right, mirrored arm waves from low to shoulder height, then they swap places.
8.0–9.0s: Quick foot switch, strong downward arm hit, a playful look back over the shoulder at the camera.
9.0–10.0s: Rise sweeping both arms diagonally up, a sharp head turn with hair movement, and neon light flares across their faces.

TRANSITION: Hard cut on the beat to a rooftop, with a small camera push-through for continuity.

SCENE 3 (10–15s): Rooftop at blue hour, city lights glowing behind
Soft blue twilight sky, glittering skyline, gentle breeze moving hair and clothing.
10.0–11.0s: The music breaks down. Slow confident walk toward the camera, hips swaying lightly on each beat.
11.0–12.0s: Smooth finger-snap isolations and a gentle hair flip, with shared eye contact between the two.
12.0–13.0s: Music snaps back. Fast synchronized combo: arm cross, open, shoulder hit, jump-step.
13.0–14.0s: Both step into a symmetrical formation: one hand on the waist, the other extended outward.
14.0–15.0s: Final confident pose toward camera, playful smile and strong eye contact, a brief freeze on the last beat.

CAMERA:
Scene 1: low-to-medium full-body framing, subtle forward tracking, then a small lateral slide.
Scene 2: handheld micro-movement, tracking alongside the dancers, subtle push-in on the drop.
Scene 3: slow arc around the dancers, ending with a short cinematic hold.
Smooth and physically realistic. No excessive shaking, no random zoom. Both dancers always clearly visible. Natural depth of field, tiny lens parallax, realistic motion blur only on fast moves.

PERFORMANCE:
Clean, fast choreography with natural weight transfer, realistic foot contact, natural knee and hip articulation, believable arm momentum. Hair and clothing react naturally to movement.

LIGHTING & COLOR:
Scene 1: warm golden-hour light with soft shadows. Scene 2: neon pink and blue rim light on wet surfaces. Scene 3: cool blue-hour ambience with warm city-light highlights. Cinematic color grading, clean natural skin tones, controlled contrast, soft filmic dynamic range, premium Japanese fashion-film look.

REALISM:
Ultra-realistic live-action humans, visible skin texture and pores, individual hair strands, realistic eye reflections, physically accurate fabric and shadows.

STRICT NEGATIVE:
No plastic skin, no CGI, no anime, no 3D render, no beauty filter, no stiff or robotic movement, no facial morphing, no face replacement, no identity change, no hairstyle or outfit change, no glowing eyes, no extra fingers or limbs, no duplicated dancers, no body distortion, no text or watermarks, no teleportation.

#AIVideo #AIDance #Seedance #Tokyo #CityPop #AIArt #aigeneratedart
```

</details>

```
Create a high-energy 15-second commercial using the provided image as the exact visual reference. Keep the same girl, outfit, sunglasses, turquoise tiled booth, Fish Creative branding, and drink.

0–3 sec: Start with a rapid camera push-in toward the girl. She grabs the Fish Creative drink and immediately brings it toward the camera.

12–15 sec: Camera quickly pulls back. She raises the drink toward the camera in a bold advertising pose, ending with the drink prominently in the foreground.

Visual direction: extremely fast-paced, energetic commercial, dynamic handheld camera, rapid push-ins, whip pans, quick zooms, snap transitions, lively body movement, punchy timing, vibrant retro aesthetic, cinematic lighting, subtle film grain.

Important: No slow movements, no slow motion, no static shots, no long pauses. Keep the girl actively moving and interacting with the drink throughout the entire 15 seconds.
```

**作者:** @AIwithkhan | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2101329280651391384) | **公開日:** 2026-09-19 | **ヒート:** 48

**安定度:** 73/100

**再テスト:** Grok Imagine · 2026-10-03 · ⚠️ 劣化 (スコア 73.3) · [出力](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-a-girl-enters-a-small-late-night-medical-drug-store-holding-a-prescription-tir-dc48c521bdae/video-grok-imagine-20261003-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（27 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
A girl enters a small late-night medical drug store holding a prescription, tired and slightly anxious; realistic Korean horror, narrow aisles filled with medicine boxes, glass cabinets, cold fluorescent lighting, quiet empty atmosphere.
She approaches the counter and asks the pharmacist for her medicine while he searches the shelves.
Her fingers freeze around the prescription; repeated scanner beeps echo through the silent store as the fluorescent lights begin flickering.
She suddenly stares toward the medicine aisle, sensing something behind her.
Her fingers begin twitching uncontrollably, slowly curling into unnatural positions.
She violently convulses against the counter; the pharmacist stumbles backward as medicine bottles fall from the shelves.
She collapses behind the counter, one arm twitching at an unnatural angle, her limbs becoming rigid and distorted.
She slowly rises again with wide unfocused eyes, a slack face, bent joints, and unnaturally twisted limbs.
Her head tilts at a broken angle as she stares directly at the pharmacist without blinking.
The pharmacist backs away in terror as her arms suddenly bend backward at the elbows.
Her jaw stretches impossibly wide, revealing an unnatural scream that echoes through the pharmacy.
She suddenly lunges forward over the counter with explosive speed, forcing the pharmacist to run.
She vaults over the counter with backward-bending limbs, moving unnaturally fast between the narrow aisles.
She stands completely still between the medicine shelves, wide unfocused eyes staring toward the entrance.
A customer enters and freezes when she slowly turns her head toward them.
Her fingers twitch as her bent joints and distorted limbs begin moving again.
She suddenly lunges down the aisle, knocking medicine boxes and bottles from the shelves.
The customer runs toward the exit and trips over a fallen basket.
The pharmacist grabs a fire extinguisher and pulls the pin with shaking hands.
White powder blasts into her face; she recoils into a glass medicine cabinet, her limbs folding at unnatural angles.
She slowly rises from the cloud, powder covering her face, wide unfocused eyes locked on the pharmacist.
Her jaw drops and stretches impossibly wide again as she suddenly lunges toward him.
He sprays the extinguisher continuously while backing toward the entrance.
She crashes through the glass door, her bent limbs twisting unnaturally as shards scatter across the empty nighttime street.
The pharmacist and customer stare through the shattered doorway in silence.
Final CCTV angle from inside the medical store; empty aisles, flickering lights, scattered medicine boxes.
A single medicine box slowly falls from a shelf. Cut to black.
```

</details>

### Gym selfie set unravels when she spots her crush and drops a dumbbell

> Gym Crush Interrupts her Set Vlog Made with Seedance 2.5 on @openart_ai prompt: **CAMERA:** DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld feel. POV of CHASE holding the camer…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/1210874751b5.jpg" width="600" alt="Gym selfie set unravels when she spots her crush and drops a dumbbell">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-dv-16mm-tape-camcorder-handheld-feel-7af91752e10c?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @doctorwasif | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/doctorwasif/status/2121113184706920701) | **公開日:** 2026-11-13 | **ヒート:** 47

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（44 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
**CAMERA:**
DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld feel. POV of CHASE holding the camera herself, often propped on a bench or rack for hands-free shots. Hand shake, misaligned framing, delayed focus pulls, clumsy zooms, occasional face cut-off framing, imperfect shots. The camera jolts and snaps toward the "crush" whenever she gets distracted. Camcorder never appears on screen.

**LOOK:**
Soft, slightly blurry tape quality, faint tape noise, bloomed highlights under gym lighting, flickering auto-exposure, muted contrast, realistic skin tones. A touch of warm glow on the crush's side of the gym for comedic effect.

**STYLE:**
Playful, relatable comedy-drama. She starts focused and confident, gets distracted, tries to act cool, and spirals into small embarrassing moments she plays off with a straight face. Quick handheld cuts, whispered asides to the lens, comedic timing built on glances and awkward pauses. No big dialogue from the crush, he stays a background figure.

**Characters**

CHASE: Korean idol, 20s. Long black hair in a high ponytail, glowing skin with a light sweat sheen, big expressive eyes. Slim athletic build. Modest long-sleeve athletic top, loose joggers or fitted leggings (arms and torso fully covered), sneakers, towel around neck, no jewelry.

THE CRUSH: fictional young man, 20s, fit and casually good-looking, plain gym t-shirt and joggers, headphones on, completely unaware of her. Seen mostly in the background, out of focus or at a distance.

**Setting**

A gym in the early evening: dumbbell rack, mirror wall, a bench, cable machines two stations away, water bottle on the floor, soft overhead lighting, a few other gym-goers in the background.

**Storyboard (30s, 12 cuts)**

1. *(~3s, propped camera, medium shot)* She sits on the bench with a dumbbell, focused and confident, talking to the lens. CHASE: "Okay, shoulder day, I'm locked in today."

2. *(~2.5s, medium propped shot)* She starts a set of dumbbell presses, steady and controlled. CHASE (counting under her breath): "Seven... eight..."

3. *(~2s, handheld, sudden whip pan)* The camera snaps toward the cable machines, where THE CRUSH walks in, headphones on, out of focus. No dialogue, soft ambient gym sound only.

4. *(~2.5s, close handheld, her face)* Her eyes dart, then freeze. She keeps pressing but whispers to the lens. CHASE (whispering): "Okay. Don't look. Don't look."

5. *(~2.5s, medium propped shot)* She tries to look casual, flexing her arm slightly and adjusting her ponytail. CHASE (whispering): "I am very normal right now."

6. *(~2s, macro insert, shallow DOF)* Her hand gripping the dumbbell, knuckles tight, the weight beginning to wobble. No dialogue, ambient gym sound only.

7. *(~2.5s, handheld, the drop)* She glances over one more time and the dumbbell slips, hitting the floor with a loud thud. The camera jolts. CHASE: "Oh— no—"

8. *(~2s, close handheld, her face)* She freezes, eyes wide, then slowly looks toward the crush. He doesn't turn around (headphones). No dialogue, heavy awkward silence.

9. *(~2.5s, medium propped shot)* She picks up the dumbbell with exaggerated casualness, then pretends to inspect it. CHASE (whispering): "Yep. Just checking the weight. Totally intentional."

10. *(~2.5s, handheld, comedic beat)* She sneaks a glance over her shoulder. The crush is calmly adjusting a cable, still oblivious. She exhales in relief and slumps. CHASE (whispering): "He didn't even see it."

11. *(~2.5s, close handheld)* A random gym-goer nearby gives her a slow, amused look. She stares back deadpan. CHASE: "...Okay, he didn't see it."

12. *(~3s, arm's-length selfie finish)* She leans into the lens with a mock-serious face, towel over her shoulder. CHASE: "We are never speaking of this. See you guys."
```

</details>

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/25b618393c56.jpg" width="600" alt="Blonde girl in a British pleated skirt on a London street">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-59be82fbd3d9?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @liyue_ai | **出典:** [元投稿](https://x.com/liyue_ai/status/2120758499009311063) | **公開日:** 2026-11-12 | **ヒート:** 47

<details>
<summary><b>プロンプト全文（21 行、クリックで展開）</b></summary>

```
参考上传的视频，将视频中的人物替换成图片里的角色，同时要求将视频里人物的服装换成不同款式，具有英伦风的百褶裙，替换后人物的表情要求与参考视频里的一致，场景也更换成英伦风的街头场景。然后输出视频

从指令可以看到，我只是替换了一下人物和背景，过了几分钟它就直接给了我一个复刻好可以直接发布的视频。

后来才发现它这个功能是接入了一整套Agent工作流。

- 用目前最强模型Fable 5.2 根据上传的视频抽帧对比，

- 用GPT Image 2.5根据我上传的Malfoy三视图生成参考图和场景

- 根据上传的参考视频和生成的参考图作为Seedance 2.5的参考素材，加上对应的提示词再成片。

整个过程全部由这个Agent自动完成，我只需要检查是否合格就行。

除了做爆款复刻，YouArt Desktop还可以自动剪辑长视频，制作教程视频或动效，甚至还可以连上你的社交账号帮你找选题，做视频替你发布。

想做AI视频的兄弟不妨下载YouArt Desktop体验一下。
地址：https://t.co/2yYxTFxsBf

Seedance 2.5完整提示词见评论区：
#Malfoy
```

```
image = Main Subject. Preserve her exact identity, face, hairstyle, skin tone, body proportions, natural underarm hair, olive-green faded crop top, loose high-waist light-blue jeans, black canvas sneakers, black cord necklace, wavy black hair, side-swept bangs and messy ponytail throughout.

Authentic Indonesian woman, early-2000s atmosphere. Ordinary quiet Indonesian residential neighborhood: narrow concrete alley, simple single-story houses, front terrace, two large trees, potted plants, parked motorcycles and tangled overhead cables. No shops, markets, vendors or commercial activity.

STYLE: Extremely raw handheld early-2000s DV camcorder home video. 30-SECOND CONTINUOUS HOME-VIDEO SEQUENCE — 16:9

- 20–24: Side-angle shot of her wiping the motorcycle seat, briefly glancing at the camera with a small smile.
- 24–27: She rinses and wrings the rag over the bucket.
- 27–30: She wipes her wet hands on her jeans, looks up and smiles naturally. Abrupt cut to black.

AUDIO: No music, dialogue or artificial sound. Only natural ambience: morning birds, light breeze, distant motorcycles, neighborhood chatter, rustling leaves, hammock creaks, Nokia keypad clicks, splashing water and rag squeaking against leather.

STRICT: No posing, no fashion-commercial look, no perfect composition, no stabilization, no cinematic shots, no artificial lighting, no modern color grading. Maintain exact character consistency in every frame.
```

```
Create a photorealistic Hollywood heist thriller set in a luxurious Manhattan art gallery at night. 16:9, 24 fps, fast-paced suspense, realistic action, dynamic camera movement, warm lighting, and natural 35mm film grain.

Character A, matching the reference image, wears an elegant black evening outfit and enters a glamorous exhibition where a priceless diamond is displayed under glass. While a waiter distracts the guests, she secretly steals it. Security discovers the missing diamond and chases her through crowded galleries, modern sculptures, and glass corridors overlooking New York City.

Cornered at the grand staircase, she calmly reveals the diamond and surrenders it. As guards secure the jewel, she notices a wealthy collector fleeing the gallery. A curator discovers evidence that an exhibited painting is the original artwork stolen by the collector years earlier. Security stops him while Character A quietly slips toward the exit.

In the final shot, she smiles knowingly through the glass doors and disappears into the night. The diamond was only a distraction; recovering the stolen painting was her real mission.

Continuity: Keep all faces, outfits, props, and locations consistent. Use clear action geography, believable movements, quick cuts, tracking shots, reflections, and close-ups. Natural gallery ambience, footsteps, glass clinks, radio chatter, and minimal dialogue. No music, narration, subtitles, text, logos, or watermarks. Avoid cartoon physics, teleportation, exaggerated action, and visual inconsistencies.
```

```
Prompt 👇 
Create a heartwarming cinematic 3D animated scene in a warm, cozy family living room during golden hour. A cute toddler girl with curly light-brown hair, wearing a simple white sleeveless dress and white socks, stands shyly behind a sheer white curtain near a large sunlit window. She looks playful and curious, gently peeking through the curtain.

Cut to her young father, a handsome brown-haired man wearing a light beige button-up shirt with rolled sleeves and blue jeans, sitting in the living room. He suddenly notices his little daughter and looks surprised and concerned. He quickly gets up from the sofa, moves around it, and searches for her with expressive facial reactions.

The little girl laughs joyfully with her eyes closed while her father looks at her lovingly. He hugs her close and spins gently in place. No text, no subtitles, no watermark, no distorted hands, no extra fingers, no duplicate characters, consistent character identity throughout.
```

```
Scene 5 (16-20s) — Sledding Adventure: She sits on a wooden sled at the top of a gentle hill, pushes off, and speeds down laughing, hair flying loose from under her beanie. Scene 8 (27-30s) — Fireside Night Reflection: A cabin interior at night, fireplace glowing, snow visible through a frosted window behind her. Final intimate close-up as she looks directly into the lens with a soft, warm smile, saying, "Goodnight from the mountains."
```

```
Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI 

Prompt

Create a 30-second, 16:9 ultra-realistic handheld lifestyle vlog following the same young Japanese woman in her 20s through an ordinary afternoon in Kyoto. Keep her face, identity, hairstyle, body, outfit, accessories, and proportions perfectly consistent in every shot.

She wears a tight pastel green halter top, straight-leg light denim jeans, yellow canvas sneakers, a small tan tote bag, and thin gold hoop earrings throughout.

New scenario: A quiet afternoon in Kyoto

0–5s — Leaving home

She steps out of a small traditional Kyoto townhouse into a quiet residential lane. She checks the sky, adjusts the strap of her tote bag, and notices the camera. She gives a small smile and says naturally:

「今日は、ゆっくりしよう。」

She starts walking, and the camera follows a little late.

5–11s — Small flower shop

She stops at a tiny neighborhood flower shop, looks at a bucket of fresh flowers, and gently touches a small yellow flower. She smiles and says:

「この色、好きだな。」

She buys one small flower, places it carefully in her tote bag, and walks outside. The camera briefly loses focus on her hands before finding her face again.

11–17s — Quiet café

She enters a small local café, orders an iced tea, and sits near the window. She looks outside at the passing bicycles and old wooden buildings, then turns toward the camera and says softly:

「こういう時間、落ち着くね。」

She takes a sip, notices the camera still filming, and gives a slightly amused smile.

17–23s — Narrow walking street

She leaves the café and walks through a narrow street lined with small shops. A delivery bicycle passes unexpectedly, and she steps aside with a little laugh. She looks back at the camera and says:

「あ、びっくりした。」

She continues walking, holding her tote bag naturally at her side.

23–27s — Canal-side pause

She reaches a quiet canal-side path in the late afternoon. She sits on a low stone ledge, places her drink beside her, and takes the small flower out of her tote bag. She looks at it for a moment, then watches the water. The wind moves her hair naturally, and she gives a small, genuine smile.

27–30s — Walking home

She stands, puts the flower back into her tote bag, and starts walking away. After a few steps, she looks back at the camera and says:

「また、ゆっくり歩こうね。」

She continues walking as the camera follows behind. Recording naturally ends around 29.5 seconds.

Filming style

Genuine footage filmed casually by a close friend on a smartphone or compact camera. Handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, exposure pumping, natural motion blur, mild digital noise, occasional late camera reactions. No gimbal, drone, cinematic choreography, slow motion, beauty filter, or commercial look.

Camcorder aesthetic

Authentic early-2000s MiniDV / consumer camcorder footage, as if someone found an old family tape from around 2002–2005.

4:3 home-video framing

Slight handheld shake

Imperfect framing

Autofocus hunting

Occasional exposure pumping

Mild blown highlights

Soft digital sharpness

Interlaced-looking motion characteristics

Subtle motion blur

Low dynamic range

Faded, slightly warm colors

Realistic digital noise

Tiny compression artifacts

Occasional accidental micro-zoom

Imperfect white balance

Slight lens flare when sunlight hits the lens

Natural camera operator mistakes

Do not make the footage excessively degraded. It should still be clear enough to see her face and environment.

Realism

The most important goal is believability.

Accurate human biomechanics, grounded feet, continuous objects, realistic walking, sitting, drinking, and handling of the flower. Consistent architecture and wardrobe. Hands must remain anatomically correct with five fingers. Clothes must move naturally with her body and wind.
```

</details>

```
[ENGINE DIRECTIVE]
Generate a continuous 30-second hyper-realistic, high-scale sci-fi mech action sequence at 60fps in 16:9 widescreen format. Style: Hollywood blockbuster sci-fi cinema (Pacific Rim scale meets extreme ballistics), rapid 2-to-3 second beats, extreme camera tracking velocity, 120fps macro impact pauses, violent kinetic physics, zero idle downtime. Maintain 100% strict visual consistency with provided asset sheets: "Vanguard" (40-foot dark tungsten bio-mechanical titan with single blue optic and 15-meter rail cannon) and "Apex" (40-foot dark bronze/black rival titan behind stone cover). Setting strictly follows "Sun-Scorched Desert Badlands".

[AUDIO DIRECTIVE - PURE SFX / ZERO MUSIC]
• SOUND DESIGN: Mechanical optic focus clicks, booming kinetic cannon roar, steam vent hiss, heavy brass shell impact clang, supersonic air shear, exploding granite shatter, screeching metal deformation, sub-bass impact drop, 500m sonic boom, and cooling metal ticks. NO BACKGROUND MUSIC.

[TIMELINE BREAKDOWN]

00:00 - 00:02 | STOP-SCROLLING HOOK: BINOCULAR TARGET LOCK
• VISUAL: Extreme close-up inside a multi-lens binocular optic array.
• ACTION: FRAME 0 HOOK. Reticles adjust instantly. Five miles across the sun-scorched desert, target locking graphics acquire Apex crouching behind a 10-foot granite fortress peak.
• SOUND: Mechanical optic focus clicks, high-frequency radar sweep hum, deep desert wind.

00:02 - 00:05 | HYDRAULIC RECOIL & FIRING IMPACT
• VISUAL: Low-angle crash-zoom on Vanguard pulling the colossal rail trigger.
• ACTION: The 15-meter Rail-Sniper Cannon fires—a violent concussive shockwave blasts surrounding sand dunes 100 feet outward in a circular wave. Hydraulic dampeners vent steam as Vanguard's massive arm absorbs the recoil.
• SOUND: Booming kinetic cannon roar, sharp mechanical steam vent, heavy sub-bass crater THUD.

00:05 - 00:08 | ARTILLERY CASING EJECT & HYPERSONIC TRAIL
• VISUAL: Extreme macro angle tracking the ejecting casing and muzzle ignition.
• ACTION: A glowing, red-hot bullet casing—the size of an artillery shell—ejects with a heavy clatter onto desert rock. A hypersonic plasma trail ignites from the muzzle, cutting through heat haze.
• SOUND: Heavy brass-on-rock impact clang, sizzling heat hiss, low-frequency sonic hum.

00:08 - 00:11 | HYPERSONIC TRAJECTORY & SPIRE SLICING
• VISUAL: Tracking camera riding beside the massive sabot round at Mach 7.
• ACTION: The round cuts through the air, shearing through a towering sandstone spire mid-flight, splitting the rock effortlessly in half while pushing a cone of compressed air and desert dust behind it.
• SOUND: Whipping supersonic air-shear, crumbling rock detonation, rushing wind tunnel roar.

00:11 - 00:14 | FIVE-MILE WHIP-ZOOM CLOSING GAP
• VISUAL: Hyper-fast whip-zoom camera flight following projectile trajectory.
• ACTION: The camera closes the 5-mile gap in seconds over sand dunes, canyon cracks, and ruined pillars directly toward the target zone.
• SOUND: Rising pitch sonic whistle, rapid camera wind WHOOSH.

00:14 - 00:17 | 120FPS GRANITE COVER PENETRATION
• VISUAL: 120 FPS slow-motion macro shot on the stone fortress cover.
• ACTION: The sniper round strikes the 10-foot granite cover behind which Apex hides. The stone wall instantly shatters into thousands of flying rock fragments as the heavy tungsten core punches clean through.
• SOUND: Explosive stone shatter, high-velocity projectile impact RING.

00:17 - 00:20 | FULL-CONTACT ARMOR DEFORMATION
• VISUAL: Extreme macro close-up on Apex's bio-mechanical chest plating.
• ACTION: The sabot round slams into Apex's outer chest armor. Heavy tungsten plates buckle under sheer kinetic force, showering sparks and pressurized hydraulic fluid outward as the projectile breaches the main chassis.
• SOUND: Screeching metal deformation, explosive armor breach CRUNCH, deep sub-bass drop.

00:20 - 00:23 | FULL OBLITERATION & RUBBLE BLAST
• VISUAL: Close-up action shot capturing the destruction of the fortress pillar.
• ACTION: The kinetic transfer of the round completely disintegrates Apex and the top half of the stone pillar, blowing heavy debris, gear, and metallic chassis components outward into desert air.
• SOUND: Thunderous explosive blast, crushing metal tearing, falling rubble RATTLE.

00:23 - 00:27 | 500-METER AERIAL SHOCKWAVE EXPANSION
• VISUAL: High-altitude bird's-eye aerial view looking down at the desert floor.
• ACTION: A massive, perfectly circular 360-degree kinetic shockwave expands 500 meters across the desert, blowing sand dunes and dust clouds outward from the impact site.
• SOUND: Echoing atmospheric sonic boom, sweeping dust storm rumble.

00:27 - 00:30 | HERO STANCE & DISTANT MUSHROOM DUST
• VISUAL: Cinematic wide shot back on Vanguard five miles away under the desert sun.
• ACTION: Vanguard lowers his smoking 15-meter rail cannon; heat haze shimmers around the barrel as a faint mushroom cloud of dust rises on the distant horizon. CUT TO BLACK at 00:30.
• SOUND: Fading desert wind, cooling metal ticking, heavy mechanical breath exhale, total silence.
```
